 function along(v,s){const P=v.path;for(let i=1;i<P.length;i++){const a=P[i-1],b=P[i],l=Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.y-a.y);if(s<=l||i===P.length-1){const f=l>0?Math.min(1,Math.max(0,s/l)):1;return{x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*f,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*f}}s-=l}return P[P.length-1]}
 const VDUR={lift:2.6,down:1.8,gate:1.1,up:1.5,dn:1.1};
 function visTick(now){const v=VIS.v;if(!v)return false;
  if(!walk()||State.taxiRide||State.ferryRide){visEnd(false);return false}
  const t=(now-v.t0)/1000,go=ph=>{v.ph=ph;v.t0=now};

   if(s>=v.L){PlayerPed.moving=false;if(v.ph==='in'){go(v.kind==='roof'?'lift':'gate');v.camOn=v.kind==='roof'}else{visEnd(true);return true}}return true}
  PlayerPed.moving=false;v.hold={x:PlayerPed.x,y:PlayerPed.y};
  if(v.ph==='lift'){v.hide=true;v.camOn=true;if(t>=VDUR.lift){go('on');try{toast('על הגג של מגדל הכיכר · המשקפת · E — לרדת')}catch(e){}}}
  else if(v.ph==='gate'){v.hide=false;if(t>=VDUR.gate){go('up');v.camOn=true}}

  else if(v.ph==='on'){v.hide=true;if(v.kind==='stand'&&v.match){let on=true;try{on=plcMatchOn()}catch(e){}if(!on&&!v.endAt){v.endAt=now+2500;try{toast('השריקה — המשחק נגמר, יוצאים מהיציע')}catch(e){}}if(v.endAt&&now>=v.endAt)visOut(now)}}
  else if(v.ph==='down'){v.hide=true;if(t>=VDUR.down){go('back');v.camOn=false;v.hold=null}}

 function visOut(now){const v=VIS.v;if(!v)return false;const T=now||pnow(),f=Math.min(1,(T-v.t0)/1000/(VDUR[v.ph]||1));

 const SAP_KEY={R:{a:.24,s:.19,z:.98,lean:.04,drop:0,pitch:.05},G:{a:.46,s:.17,z:.52,lean:.5,drop:.2,pitch:.42},H:{a:.3,s:.16,z:1.1,lean:.02,drop:0,pitch:.1},
  P:{a:.55,s:.14,z:1.05,lean:.14,drop:.02,pitch:.12},T:{a:.44,s:.17,z:.86,lean:.24,drop:.06,pitch:.32},C:{a:.22,s:.1,z:1.52,lean:0,drop:0,pitch:-.04},U:{a:.22,s:.22,z:1.78,lean:-.04,drop:0,pitch:-.3}};
  
 const SAP_ACT={saDn:['2','R','G',6],saUp:['2','G','H',6],saDnT:['2','R','T',6],saUpT:['2','T','H',6],saGive:['2','H','P',6],saCam:['2','C','C',1],saWork:['2','T','T',8,1],saFix:['2','P','P',8,1],saPick:['2','R','G',8,2],saHang:['2','H','U',6],
  saShake:['1',u=>({a:.42,s:.04,z:1+.035*Math.sin(u*TAU*2)}),8],saTalk:['1',u=>({a:.3+.06*Math.sin(u*TAU),s:.26,z:1.14+.07*Math.sin(u*TAU+1.2)}),8],saPhone:['1',()=>({a:.08,s:.12,z:1.62}),1],
  saWave:['1',u=>({a:.14,s:.3+.1*Math.sin(u*TAU),z:1.86}),8],saPay:['1',()=>({a:.5,s:.06,z:1.08}),1],saStamp:['1',u=>({a:.42,s:.12,z:.92+.12*Math.abs(Math.sin(u*Math.PI))}),8],
  saWrite:['W',u=>({a:.32+.02*Math.sin(u*TAU*2),s:.02,z:1.1+.015*Math.cos(u*TAU*2)}),8]};
 const sapF=k=>{const A=SAP_ACT[k];return A?(A[0]==='2'?A[3]:A[2])||1:1};
 function sapPose(S,L,act,fr,X){const A=SAP_ACT[act.k],F=sapF(act.k),{comb,ik,add,sub,mul,nrm,Z}=X,D=S.D,f=S.f,l=S.l,O=[0,0,0],hk=D.h/1.76,u=F>1?fr/F:0;
  const arm=(side,tg,fU,lU,bob)=>{const sh=add(add(S.torsoAt(D.shZ+bob),[0,0,D.shZ+bob]),add(mul(lU,side*D.armS),mul(fU,-.008))),grip=comb(O,f,tg.a*hk,l,side*tg.s*hk,tg.z*hk),wd0=nrm(sub(grip,sh)),wrT=sub(grip,mul(wd0,.05*D.hs));
   const j=ik(sh,wrT,D.uarm,D.farm,nrm(add(add(mul(lU,side*.7),[0,0,-1]),mul(fU,-.3)))),wr=j.ank,wd=nrm(sub(grip,wr));return{side,sh,el:j.knee,wr,hand:add(wr,mul(wd,.052*D.hs)),wd}};
  if(A[0]!=='2'){const bob=S.dz||0,R=arm(-1,A[1](u),S.fU,S.lU,bob),Lf=A[0]==='W'?arm(1,{a:.3,s:.06,z:1.06},S.fU,S.lU,bob):S.arms[0];S.arms=[Lf,R];return}
   
  const t=A[4]===2?.5-.5*Math.cos(u*TAU):F>1?Math.min(1,fr/(F-1)):0,k0=SAP_KEY[A[1]],k1=SAP_KEY[A[2]],m=x=>k0[x]+(k1[x]-k0[x])*t,osc=A[4]===1?Math.sin(u*TAU):0;
  const lean=m('lean'),pitch=m('pitch'),pelZ=D.hipZ-m('drop')*hk,pel=comb(O,f,-lean*.12,l,0,pelZ),toeA=(L.shoes&&L.shoes.t==='leather'?.082:.072)*D.hs;S.pel=pel;S.legs=[];
  for(const side of [1,-1]){const hip=comb(pel,f,0,l,side*D.hipS,0),ank=comb(O,f,side>0?.06:-.05,l,side*(D.hipS+.04),D.ankZ),j=ik(hip,ank,D.thigh,D.shin,f),
   toe=add(j.ank,add(mul(f,toeA),[0,0,-D.ankZ+.028])),heel=add(j.ank,add(mul(f,-.028*D.hs),[0,0,-D.ankZ+.03]));toe[2]=Math.max(.025,toe[2]);heel[2]=Math.max(.028,heel[2]);S.legs.push({side,hip,knee:j.knee,ank:j.ank,toe,heel,y:l[1]*side})}
  const fU=S.fU,lU=S.lU,tc=comb(O,fU,-lean*.1,lU,0,0);S.torsoAt=z=>comb(tc,fU,lean*(z-pelZ),lU,0,0);S.pelZ=pelZ;S.lean=lean;const bob=pelZ-D.hipZ;S.dz=bob;
  S.neck=[add(S.torsoAt(D.neck0+bob),[0,0,D.neck0+bob]),add(S.torsoAt(D.neck1+bob),[fU[0]*.012,fU[1]*.012,D.neck1+bob])];
  const ef=nrm(add(mul(fU,Math.cos(pitch)),mul(Z,-Math.sin(pitch)))),ez=nrm(add(mul(Z,Math.cos(pitch)),mul(fU,Math.sin(pitch))));
  S.head={c:add(add(S.torsoAt(D.headZ+bob),[0,0,D.headZ+bob]),add(mul(fU,.016*D.hs),mul(ef,.01*D.hs))),ax:[lU,ef,ez],r:D.head};

 function clearTo(x0,y0,x1,y1){const L=Math.hypot(x1-x0,y1-y0),n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(L/6)),bl=(x,y)=>{try{return!!personBlockedAt(x,y,4)}catch(e){return false}};let last={x:x0,y:y0},esc=bl(x0,y0);

 function standAt(P,d){const a0=Math.atan2(PlayerPed.y-P.y,PlayerPed.x-P.x);for(const da of[0,.6,-.6,1.2,-1.2,1.9,-1.9,Math.PI]){const a=a0+da,q={x:P.x+Math.cos(a)*d,y:P.y+Math.sin(a)*d};let b=false;try{b=!!personBlockedAt(q.x,q.y,4)}catch(e){}if(!b)return q}return{x:PlayerPed.x,y:PlayerPed.y}}
 
  
 function walkPath(a,to){const e=clearTo(a.x,a.y,to.x,to.y);if(Math.hypot(e.x-to.x,e.y-to.y)<=3)return[a,to];const dx=to.x-a.x,dy=to.y-a.y,L=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,ux=-dy/L,uy=dx/L;
  for(const k of[.5,.3,.7])for(const off of[24,-24,42,-42,64,-64,84,-84]){const m={x:a.x+dx*k+ux*off,y:a.y+dy*k+uy*off},p=clearTo(a.x,a.y,m.x,m.y);if(Math.hypot(p.x-m.x,p.y-m.y)>2)continue;const r=clearTo(m.x,m.y,to.x,to.y);if(Math.hypot(r.x-to.x,r.y-to.y)<=3){E.detour=(E.detour||0)+1;return[a,m,to]}}
  return[a,e]}
 function psRun(steps,o){if(PS.q)psStop('replace');PS.q={steps,i:-1,o:o||{},t0:pnow(),seen:[],dist:0,t:0};PS.n++;psNext(pnow());return PS.q}
 function faceOf(s){const F=typeof s.face==='function'?s.face():s.face;if(F&&Number.isFinite(F.x)){s.hd=Math.atan2(F.y-PlayerPed.y,F.x-PlayerPed.x);PlayerPed.heading=s.hd;try{PlayerPed.dir=personDirection(Math.cos(s.hd),Math.sin(s.hd))}catch(e){}}}
 function psNext(now){const q=PS.q;if(!q)return;if(q.i>=0)q.t+=now-q.t0;q.i++;q.t0=now;const s=q.steps[q.i];

  q.seen.push(s.k==='pose'?(s.p||'car'):s.k);{const L=PS.log||(PS.log=[]);L.push([q.o.tag,s.k==='pose'?(s.p||'car'):s.k,now|0,PlayerPed.x|0,PlayerPed.y|0]);if(L.length>80)L.shift()}if(s.k==='pose'&&s.p)PS.poses[s.p]=(PS.poses[s.p]||0)+1;

  if(s.k==='walk'){const to=typeof s.to==='function'?s.to():s.to;if(!to||!walk())return psNext(now);s.pts=walkPath({x:PlayerPed.x,y:PlayerPed.y},to);s.L=0;for(let i=1;i<s.pts.length;i++)s.L+=Math.hypot(s.pts[i].x-s.pts[i-1].x,s.pts[i].y-s.pts[i-1].y);if(s.L<2)return psNext(now);s.hd=Math.atan2(s.pts[1].y-s.pts[0].y,s.pts[1].x-s.pts[0].x)}
  if(s.k==='pose'){PlayerPed.moving=false;if(s.face)faceOf(s);if(q.o.fig)psFig(q.o.fig,s.fig||null)}

 function psTick(now){const q=PS.q;if(!q)return;if(VIS.v||State.ferryRide||State.taxiRide&&State.taxiRide.phase==='riding'){psStop('busy');return}if(cutOnly()){q.t0+=Math.max(0,now-(q.lt||now));q.lt=now;return}q.lt=now;    
  const s=q.steps[q.i];if(!s){psNext(now);return}const t=Math.max(0,(now-q.t0)/1000);
  if(s.k==='walk'){if(!walk()){psStop('mode');return}const d=Math.min(s.L,t*(s.sp||WSP));let r=d,x=s.pts[0].x,y=s.pts[0].y;for(let i=1;i<s.pts.length;i++){const A=s.pts[i-1],B=s.pts[i],l=Math.hypot(B.x-A.x,B.y-A.y);if(r<=l||i===s.pts.length-1){const f=l>0?Math.min(1,r/l):1;x=A.x+(B.x-A.x)*f;y=A.y+(B.y-A.y)*f;if(l>0)s.hd=Math.atan2(B.y-A.y,B.x-A.x);break}r-=l}q.dist+=Math.hypot(x-PlayerPed.x,y-PlayerPed.y);

 function actSteps(c){const P=c.pt,S=[],D=c.dur/1000,desk=c.o==='paper'||c.o==='small',up=desk?'saUpT':'saUp',dn=desk?'saDnT':'saDn',face=()=>c.k==='look'&&c.tg?c.tg:c.k==='via'?c.poi:P;
  const go=(p,d,o)=>Object.assign({k:'pose',p,d:Math.max(.35,d),face,on:()=>{c.phase='work'}},o||{}),pr=(a,b)=>f=>{c.prog=a+(b-a)*f},m=me(),far=Math.hypot(m.x-P.x,m.y-P.y);

  else if(c.k==='meet'){}
  else if(c.k!=='sort'&&(!(c.k==='look'||c.k==='photo')||far>60))S.push({k:'walk',to:()=>standAt(P,['dev','board','rack','flag','pole','post','tripod'].includes(c.o)?8:7)});
  switch(c.k){
   case'take':case'find':S.push(go(dn,D*.5,{prog:pr(0,.54)}),go(up,D*.5,{prog:pr(.56,1),held:1}));break;
   case'give':{S.push(go(up,D*.55,{prog:pr(0,.54),held:1,rev:1}),go(dn,D*.45,{prog:pr(.56,1),rev:1}));const f=figFor(c.id,c.n);if(f&&Math.hypot(f.pt.x-P.x,f.pt.y-P.y)<220)S.push({k:'walk',to:()=>standAt(f.pt,9)},...talkSteps(f,'בבקשה — '+c.nm,null,true));c.fig=f;break}
   case'pick':case'plant':S.push(go('saPick',D,{cyc:.9,prog:pr(0,1)}));break;
   case'work':{const fx=c.opt.fx,p=fx==='stamp'?'saStamp':fx==='radio'?'saPhone':fx==='marshal'?'marshal':['dev','flag','post','tripod','pole'].includes(c.o)?'saFix':'saWork';S.push(go(p,D,{cyc:p==='marshal'?1.6:.8,prog:pr(0,1)}));break}
   case'check':S.push(go('saWrite',D,{cyc:.9,prog:pr(0,1)}));break;
   case'photo':S.push(go('saCam',D,{prog:pr(0,1)}));break;
   case'look':S.push(go('saCam',D,{prog:f=>{c.prog=f;if(c.tg){const g=lookTgt(c);if(g)c.tg=g}},hold:now=>c.opt.tgt==='span'&&(c.cars=spanCars())>0&&now-c.t0<20000&&(c.prog=Math.min(c.prog,.95),true)}));break;

   case'hang':S.push(go('saHang',D,{prog:pr(0,1),held:1}));break;

   case'sort':{const src=c.src,dstOf=()=>c.opt.car?carBack():c.dst;for(let i=c.cnt;i<c.q;i++)S.push({k:'walk',to:()=>standAt(src,8)},go('saDn',.45,{face:()=>src,on:()=>{c.phase='work'}}),go('saUp',.45,{face:()=>src,held:1,on:()=>{c.phase='work';c.inHand=1}}),

   case'meet':{const s=ST()[c.id].steps[c.n-1],line=s.msg&&s.msg[1]?String(s.msg[1]).slice(0,130):'';

  return S}

 function clear(g,L){if(L.k!=='mv'&&L.k!=='turn')return true;const x0=Math.min(g.x,L.k==='mv'?L.x:g.x),x1=Math.max(g.x,L.k==='mv'?L.x:g.x),y0=Math.min(g.y,L.k==='mv'?L.y:g.y),y1=Math.max(g.y,L.k==='mv'?L.y:g.y);

 function stepTug(g,dt){if(!g.leg){g.leg=g.legs.shift()||null;if(g.leg)g.leg.ready=false}const L=g.leg;g.v=0;g.turning=false;if(!L)return;g.mode=L.mode||g.mode;
  if(L.k==='home'){g.x=g.home.x;g.y=g.home.y;g.h=g.home.h;g.att=null;g.line=false;g.mode='moored';g.leg=null;return}
  if(L.k==='free'){g.att=null;g.line=false;g.leg=null;return}
  if(L.k==='wait'){if(L.fn()){g.leg=null}return}
  if(L.k==='mvx'){L.k='mv';L.x=g.x;L.y=L.dy}
  if(L.k==='mvrel'){const p=rel(shipPose(),L.f,L.l);L.k='mv';L.x=p.x;L.y=p.y}
  if(!L.ready){if(!clear(g,L)){g.hold+=dt;return}L.ready=true}
  if(L.k==='turn'){let d=adiff(g.h,L.h);if(L.dir&&Math.abs(Math.abs(d)-PI)<1e-6)d=L.dir*Math.abs(d);const s=W_TURN*dt;if(Math.abs(d)<=s){g.h=L.h;g.leg=null}else{g.h+=Math.sign(d)*s;g.turning=true}ST.moveS[g.id]+=dt;return}
  if(L.k==='mv'){const dx=L.x-g.x,dy=L.y-g.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy),s=L.v*dt;if(d>.01){const hd=Math.atan2(dy,dx);g.h=L.rev?hd+PI:hd;g.h=Math.atan2(Math.sin(g.h),Math.cos(g.h))}
   g.rev=!!L.rev;if(d<=s){g.x=L.x;g.y=L.y;g.leg=null}else{g.x+=dx/d*s;g.y+=dy/d*s;g.v=L.v}ST.moveS[g.id]+=dt;return}

 function fill(arr,cap,v){const f0=Math.max(0,cap[0]-arr[0]),f1=Math.max(0,cap[1]-arr[1]),F=f0+f1;if(F<=1e-9||v<=0)return 0;const d=Math.min(v,F);arr[0]+=d*f0/F;arr[1]+=d*f1/F;return d}
 function take(arr,v){const t=arr[0]+arr[1];if(t<=1e-9||v<=0)return 0;const d=Math.min(v,t);arr[0]-=d*arr[0]/t;arr[1]-=d*arr[1]/t;return d}
 function step(dm){if(!(dm>0))return;if(dm>5)dm=5;const h=dm/60;S.pt+=dm;
   
  const want=S.ship>1&&(S.ph==='pump'||S.ph==='start'?room()>500:room()>3000);
  if(S.ph==='idle'){S.q=0;if(want&&S.pt>=1)go('connect')}
  else if(S.ph==='connect'){S.arm=Math.min(1,S.pt/DUR.connect);if(S.arm>=1)go('valves')}
  else if(S.ph==='valves'){S.valve=S.pt>=DUR.valves*.5;if(S.pt>=DUR.valves)go('start')}
  else if(S.ph==='start'){S.pump=true;S.q=Q_SHIP*Math.min(1,S.pt/DUR.start);if(S.pt>=DUR.start)go('pump')}

  else if(S.ph==='empty'){S.q=0;if(S.pt>=DUR.wait)go('refill')}
  else if(S.ph==='refill'){S.q=0;const d=Math.min(CAP.ship-S.ship,CAP.ship*dm/DUR.refill);S.ship+=d;S.bal.load+=d;if(S.ship>=CAP.ship-1e-6){S.ship=CAP.ship;S.loads++;go('idle')}}

  let r=fill(S.ref,CAP.ref,out);S.bal.inRef+=r;let b=fill(S.buf,CAP.buf,out-r);S.bal.inBuf+=b;const spill=out-r-b;if(spill>1e-6){S.ship+=spill;S.bal.out-=spill;out-=spill}

 function stepHeli(c,dt,k){if(c.spool<T.hSpool){c.spool=Math.min(T.hSpool,c.spool+dt);if(c.z<=0)return}
  const air=c.z>2;if(air)c.h=ang(c.h+((k.r?1:0)-(k.l?1:0))*T.hYaw*dt);
  const a=((k.f?1:0)-(k.b?.7:0))*T.hAcc;if(air){c.vx+=Math.cos(c.h)*a*dt;c.vy+=Math.sin(c.h)*a*dt}
   
  {const ch=Math.cos(c.h),sh=Math.sin(c.h);let fw=c.vx*ch+c.vy*sh,lt=-c.vx*sh+c.vy*ch;fw*=Math.exp(-(k.f||k.b?T.hDrag:1.2)*dt);lt*=Math.exp(-2.5*dt);c.vx=fw*ch-lt*sh;c.vy=fw*sh+lt*ch}
  let sp=Math.hypot(c.vx,c.vy);if(sp>DEF.heli.vmax){c.vx*=DEF.heli.vmax/sp;c.vy*=DEF.heli.vmax/sp;sp=DEF.heli.vmax}if(!air){c.vx=0;c.vy=0}
  const vzT=((k.up?1:0)-(k.dn?1:0))*T.hVz;c.vz+=(vzT-c.vz)*Math.min(1,3*dt);
  c.x=worldClampX(c.x+c.vx*dt);c.y=worldClampY(c.y+c.vy*dt);let z=c.z+c.vz*dt;const pad=padAt(c.x,c.y);
  if(z<=0){if(c.z>0){touch(c,{v:sp,vz:c.vz,pad})}z=0;c.vz=Math.max(0,c.vz)}else if(z<T.minZ&&!pad&&c.vz<0){z=Math.max(z,Math.min(c.z,T.minZ));c.vz=0;hint('נוחתים רק במנחת — H2 (או מנחת אחר בשדה)')}
  c.z=Math.min(DEF.heli.zmax,z);c.v=sp}
 function stepPlane(c,dt,k){c.thr=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,c.thr+((k.f?1:0)-(k.b?1:0))*T.pThr*dt));const air=c.z>0;
  const drag=c.v*c.v*(T.pAcc/(DEF.plane.vmax*DEF.plane.vmax)),roll=air?0:(c.v>0?18:0)+(k.b&&!air&&c.thr===0?260:0);c.v=Math.max(0,c.v+(c.thr*T.pAcc-drag-roll)*dt);
  const turn=(k.r?1:0)-(k.l?1:0);if(air)c.h=ang(c.h+turn*T.pYawA*dt);else if(c.v>3)c.h=ang(c.h+turn*Math.min(T.pYawG,T.pYawG*90/Math.max(90,c.v))*dt*Math.min(1,c.v/40));
  let vzT=0;if(air){vzT=((k.up?1:0)-(k.dn?1:0))*T.pVz;if(c.v<T.pStall){vzT=-160;hint('מהירות נמוכה — מוסיפים מצערת (W)')}}else if(k.up&&c.v>=T.pVR&&onRunway(c.x,c.y))vzT=T.pVz;
  c.vz+=(vzT-c.vz)*Math.min(1,2.5*dt);
  const nx=c.x+Math.cos(c.h)*c.v*dt,ny=c.y+Math.sin(c.h)*c.v*dt;
  if(!air&&vzT<=0){if(airside(nx,ny)){c.x=nx;c.y=ny}else{c.v=0;hint('המטוס נשאר בצד האווירי — מסלולי ההסעה')}c.vz=0;if(k.up&&c.v>=T.pVR&&!onRunway(c.x,c.y))hint('ממריאים רק מהמסלול')}
  else{c.x=worldClampX(nx);c.y=worldClampY(ny);let z=c.z+c.vz*dt;const rw=onRunway(c.x,c.y,10);
   if(z<=0&&c.z>0){const d=Math.min(Math.abs(ang(c.h)),Math.abs(ang(c.h-Math.PI)));if(rw&&d<T.pland.ang){touch(c,{v:c.v,vz:c.vz,rw:true});z=0;c.vz=0}else{z=T.minZ;c.vz=0;hint(rw?'מיישרים עם המסלול לפני הנגיעה':'נוחתים רק על המסלול')}}
   else if(z<T.minZ&&!rw&&c.vz<0){z=Math.max(z,Math.min(c.z,T.minZ));c.vz=0;hint('נוחתים רק על המסלול')}
   c.z=Math.max(0,Math.min(DEF.plane.zmax,z))}}
 function touch(c,o){c.touch={...o,t:CITYSVC_T()};const hard=c.kind==='heli'?(o.v>T.land.v||o.vz<T.land.vz):(o.vz<T.pland.vz);if(hard){S.st.hard++;toast('נחיתה קשה — לאט יותר (Space בעדינות)');if(S.exam&&!S.exam.res)examEnd(false,'נחיתה קשה')}
  if(c.kind==='heli'&&S.exam&&!S.exam.res&&!hard){const st=S.exam.steps[S.exam.i];if(st&&st.k==='pad'){const d=Math.hypot(c.x-st.x,c.y-st.y);if(d<=T.land.pad)examNext();else if(o.pad)examEnd(false,'נחתת במנחת '+o.pad.id+' — לא ב-H2')}else if(S.exam.i>0)examEnd(false,'נחתת באמצע המבחן')}}

 function hint(m){const t=performance.now();if(S.last&&S.last.m===m&&t-S.last.t<2500)return;S.last={m,t};try{toast(m)}catch(e){}}
  
 function examNext(){const E=S.exam;E.i++;const st=E.steps[E.i];if(!st){examEnd(true);return}try{toast('✓ '+E.steps[E.i-1].label+' · הבא: '+st.label)}catch(e){}}
 function examStep(c,dt){const E=S.exam;if(!E||E.res)return;E.t+=dt;if(E.t>E.T){examEnd(false,'הזמן נגמר');return}const st=E.steps[E.i];if(!st)return;
  if(st.k==='ring'){if(Math.hypot(c.x-st.x,c.y-st.y)<=st.r&&Math.abs(c.z-st.z)<=st.dz)examNext()}
  else if(st.k==='gate'){if(c.z<=0&&Math.hypot(c.x-st.x,c.y-st.y)<=st.r)examNext()}
  else if(st.k==='runway'){if(c.z<=0&&c.touch&&c.touch.rw&&c.v<12&&onRunway(c.x,c.y,10))examNext()}}

 function busy(kind){if(S.c&&S.c.kind===kind&&S.c.ret)return'המטוס בדרך חזרה ל-GA3';if(S.on)return'כבר בטיסה';if(PlayerProfile.activeMission)return'סיים או בטל את המשימה הפעילה קודם';if(PlayerProfile.mode!=='walk')return'יוצאים מהרכב ליד הגדר';return null}
 function start(kind,{exam=false,force=false}={}){const D=DEF[kind];if(!D)return{err:'kind'};const b=busy(kind);if(b&&!force){toast(b);return{err:b}}

   if(PlayerProfile.money<r.fee){toast('אין מספיק כסף לאגרת המבחן');return{err:'money'}}PlayerProfile.money-=r.fee;try{recordTransaction('license-exam-fee',-r.fee,{license:D.lic})}catch(e){}
   const old=PlayerProfile.licenses[D.lic]||{state:'eligible',attempts:0,bestScore:null};PlayerProfile.licenses[D.lic]={...old,state:'eligible',attempts:(old.attempts||0)+1};
   S.exam={kind,lic:D.lic,i:0,t:0,T:EXAM[kind].T,steps:EXAM[kind].steps,res:null,why:''};S.st.exams++;try{SaveManager.log('license','exam_started',{id:D.lic,fee:r.fee,vehicle:D.model})}catch(e){}}
  else{if(!lic(D.lic)){toast('צריך רישיון: '+LICENSE_TRIALS[D.lic].name);return{err:'lic'}}if(PlayerProfile.money<D.fee){toast('אין מספיק כסף לשכירת '+D.name);return{err:'money'}}PlayerProfile.money-=D.fee;try{recordTransaction('flight-rental',-D.fee,{craft:D.model})}catch(e){}S.exam=null}
  const h=D.home;S.c={kind,model:D.model,x:h.x,y:h.y,z:0,h:h.h,vx:0,vy:0,vz:0,v:0,thr:0,spool:0,t:0,dist:0,touch:null,ret:null};S.on=kind;S.st.flights++;P().flights++;

  toast((exam?'המבחן: '+LICENSE_TRIALS[D.lic].name+' · ':'')+D.name+' · '+D.where+' · '+(kind==='heli'?'Shift — עלייה, Space — ירידה, W/A/S/D — טיסה':'W — מצערת, A/D — הגה, Shift — משיכה מעל '+Math.round(kmh(T.pVR))+' קמ״ש'));

 function canExit(){const c=S.c;if(!c||!S.on)return null;if(c.kind==='heli')return c.z<=0&&Math.hypot(c.x-DEF.heli.home.x,c.y-DEF.heli.home.y)<=T.land.pad+10?null:'יורדים רק אחרי נחיתה במנחת H2';return c.z<=0&&c.v<10?null:'עוצרים קודם (S)'}

var GSVC={on:true,v:2,err:null,n:{queued:0,started:0,done:0,aborted:0,rolled:0,blocked:0,fallback:0,frames:0,lift:0,over:0,ready:0,guide:0,out:0,guideDraw:0},cur:null,last:null,out:null,outLast:null};
(function(){

 function plan(){if(State.vehicleEcoOverride||!(PlayerCar.x>-9000)||PlayerProfile.activeMission||State.licenseExam||State.taxiRide||State.ferryRide)return null;    const B=bays(),x=PlayerCar.x,y=PlayerCar.y,h=PlayerCar.heading||0;

   let len=0,p0=bez(R,0);for(let k=1;k<=16;k++){const p=bez(R,k/16);len+=Math.hypot(p.x-p0.x,p.y-p0.y);if(k<16&&blocked(p.x,p.y,p.h))return null;p0=p}R.len=len;

 function queue(old,lines){if(!GSVC.on||!lines||!lines.length)return 0;const c=GSVC.cur;if(c&&c.phase!=='queued')return 0;const p=plan();if(!p)return 0;
  const jobs=c?c.jobs.filter(j=>!lines.some(q=>q[0]===j.k)):[];for(const q of lines)jobs.push({k:q[0],label:String(q[1]||q[0])});

 function start(){GSVC.out=null;const c=GSVC.cur,p=plan();if(!p){GSVC.n.fallback++;finish('fallback');return}

  Object.assign(c,{phase:'run',T:0,total:T,segs,plan:p,neu:{c:st.c,r:st.r,v:st.v},shown:{...c.old},exp:{x:PlayerCar.x,y:PlayerCar.y},exited:false,noLift:false,z:0,lifted:false,seg:null});
  GSVC.n.started++;const lab=c.jobs.map(j=>j.label).join(' · ');
  toast('🔧 תא '+p.bay+': הרכב שלך נכנס לעבודה — '+lab+' · כ-'+Math.ceil(T)+' שנ׳ (אפשר לחכות ליד הקבלה)')}
 function tick(dt){try{tickOut(dt)}catch(e){er(e)}const c=GSVC.cur;if(!c||!GSVC.on)return;if(c.phase==='queued'){if(!State.phoneOpen)start();return}
  if(Math.hypot(PlayerCar.x-c.exp.x,PlayerCar.y-c.exp.y)>8||State.vehicleEcoOverride){GSVC.n.aborted++;finish('moved');return}
  c.T+=dt;const seg=c.segs.find(s=>c.T<s.s1)||c.segs[c.segs.length-1];c.seg=seg;
  if(seg&&seg.k==='in'){const q=bez(c.plan.roll,ease(Math.min(1,(c.T-seg.s0)/seg.dur)));PlayerCar.x=q.x;PlayerCar.y=q.y;PlayerCar.heading=q.h}
  else{const a=c.plan.at;PlayerCar.x=a.x;PlayerCar.y=a.y;PlayerCar.heading=a.h;
   if(!c.exited){c.exited=true;if(PlayerProfile.mode==='vehicle'){let ok=false;try{ok=exitVehicle()!==false&&PlayerProfile.mode==='walk'}catch(e){er(e)}if(!ok)c.noLift=true}}}
  PlayerCar.speed=0;PlayerCar.steer=0;
  for(const s of c.segs)if(s.k==='job'&&!s.done&&c.T>=s.s1){s.done=true;if(s.j.k==='c'||s.j.k==='r'||s.j.k==='v')c.shown[s.j.k]=c.neu[s.j.k]}
  const u=seg?Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(c.T-seg.s0)/seg.dur)):0;let z=0;
  if(!c.noLift&&seg){if(seg.k==='up')z=u*LIFT;else if(seg.k==='job'&&LIFTK(seg.j.k))z=LIFT;else if(seg.k==='down')z=(1-u)*LIFT}
  c.z=z;if(z>0)c.lifted=true;c.exp={x:PlayerCar.x,y:PlayerCar.y};
  if(c.T>=c.total){GSVC.n.done++;const bay=c.bay,at={...c.plan.at};finish('done');GSVC.out={bay,at,T:0,phase:'ready',tgd:null,tg:null,why:null};GSVC.n.ready++;
   toast('✓ הרכב שלך מוכן בתא '+bay+' — E לרכב; מרכיב הצמיגים יכוון אותך ביציאה: ברוורס, דרך הנמכת השפה')}}
 

 function cutK(){try{const k=D3ND.cuts.find(r=>r[1]>=2470&&r[1]<=2490&&r[0]<=4340&&r[2]>=4340);if(k)return k}catch(e){}return[4300,2482,4370,2502]}
 function exitPath(o){const a=o.at,k=cutK(),mx=(k[0]+k[2])/2;return{P0:{x:a.x,y:a.y},P1:{x:a.x,y:a.y+24},P2:{x:mx+18,y:k[1]+4},P3:{x:mx-2,y:k[3]+30}}}
 function mSpot(o){return o.bay===3?{x:4446,y:2489}:{x:4421,y:2490}}
 function mPath(o){const h=crewHome(1),s=mSpot(o);return o.bay===3?[h,{x:4446,y:2422},s]:[h,{x:h.x,y:2472},s]}
 function tickOut(dt){const o=GSVC.out;if(!o||!GSVC.on)return;o.T+=dt;const inCar=PlayerProfile.mode==='vehicle',d=Math.hypot(PlayerCar.x-o.at.x,PlayerCar.y-o.at.y);
  if(o.phase!=='gone'){let why=null;
   if(State.vehicleEcoOverride||(!inCar&&d>8))why='moved';
   else if(inCar&&(d>34||PlayerCar.y>2486))why='out';
   else if(o.phase==='ready'&&o.T>READY_MAX)why='timeout';
   else if(o.phase==='guide'&&o.T-o.tgd>GUIDE_MAX)why='timeout';
   if(why){o.phase='gone';o.tg=o.T;o.why=why;if(why==='out')GSVC.n.out++}
   else if(o.phase==='ready'&&inCar&&d<=8){o.phase='guide';o.tgd=o.T;GSVC.n.guide++}}
  if(o.phase==='gone'){const pg=o.tgd==null?0:Math.min(1,(o.tg-o.tgd)/MWALK);if(o.T-o.tg>=pg*MWALK+.1){GSVC.outLast={why:o.why,bay:o.bay,T:+o.T.toFixed(1),guided:o.tgd!=null};GSVC.out=null}}}
 function marshal(){const o=GSVC.out;if(!o||o.tgd==null)return null;const P=mPath(o);let p,back=false;
  if(o.phase==='gone'){const pg=Math.min(1,(o.tg-o.tgd)/MWALK);p=Math.max(0,pg-(o.T-o.tg)/MWALK);back=true}else p=Math.min(1,(o.T-o.tgd)/MWALK);
  if(p<=0)return null;const q=along(P,p);
  if(p<1||back)return{x:q.x,y:q.y,pose:'walk',a:back?q.a+PI:q.a,ph:(o.T*1.4)%1};
  return{x:q.x,y:q.y,pose:'marshal',a:Math.atan2(PlayerCar.y-q.y,PlayerCar.x-q.x),ph:(o.T*.9)%1,guide:true}}
  
 function guideDraw(g,N){const o=GSVC.out;if(!o||o.phase!=='guide')return;GSVC.n.guideDraw++;const R=exitPath(o),now=performance.now()/1000,n=9,f=(now*.8)%1;
  g.save();g.lineCap='round';g.lineJoin='round';
  for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const u=.22+.78*((k+f)/n);if(u>1)continue;const q=bez(R,u),cx=Math.cos(q.h),sy=Math.sin(q.h),px=-sy,py=cx,al=Math.min(1,(1-u)*4)*(N?.6:.85);
   const tip={x:q.x+cx*2.6,y:q.y+sy*2.6},l={x:q.x-cx*1.8+px*3.4,y:q.y-sy*1.8+py*3.4},r={x:q.x-cx*1.8-px*3.4,y:q.y-sy*1.8-py*3.4};
   for(const [w,col] of [[2.6,'rgba(20,20,20,'+al*.55+')'],[1.4,N?'rgba(255,214,90,'+al+')':'rgba(255,206,40,'+al+')']]){g.strokeStyle=col;g.lineWidth=w;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(l.x,l.y);g.lineTo(tip.x,tip.y);g.lineTo(r.x,r.y);g.stroke()}}
  g.restore()}
  
 function outSign(g){const o=GSVC.out;if(!o||o.phase==='gone')return;const a=o.at;try{if(!e3InView(a.x-60,a.y-80,120,120,30))return}catch(e){}
  const txt=o.phase==='guide'?'תא '+o.bay+' · יציאה ברוורס — לפי החצים':'✓ תא '+o.bay+' · הרכב שלך מוכן · E לרכב',y=a.y-34,w=Math.max(54,Math.min(110,txt.length*2.9+10));
  g.save();g.globalAlpha=.92;g.fillStyle='#151516';g.fillRect(a.x-w/2,y-5,w,10);g.fillStyle='#3f9a5a';g.fillRect(a.x-w/2,y+3.4,w,1.6);

 function crewHome(i){try{const C=window.__TC_GHALL.CREW[i];return{x:C[0],y:C[1]}}catch(e){return i?{x:4436,y:2417}:{x:4382,y:2417}}}
 function crewSpot(c,i){const a=c.plan.at,f={x:Math.cos(a.h),y:Math.sin(a.h)},s={x:Math.cos(a.h+PI/2),y:Math.sin(a.h+PI/2)},d=i?-12:8;return{x:a.x+f.x*d+s.x*17,y:a.y+f.y*d+s.y*17}}
 function along(P,u){let L=0;for(let k=1;k<P.length;k++)L+=Math.hypot(P[k].x-P[k-1].x,P[k].y-P[k-1].y);let d=u*L;for(let k=1;k<P.length;k++){const l=Math.hypot(P[k].x-P[k-1].x,P[k].y-P[k-1].y);
   if(d<=l||k===P.length-1){const v=l?Math.min(1,d/l):1;return{x:P[k-1].x+(P[k].x-P[k-1].x)*v,y:P[k-1].y+(P[k].y-P[k-1].y)*v,a:Math.atan2(P[k].y-P[k-1].y,P[k].x-P[k-1].x)}}d-=l}return{x:P[0].x,y:P[0].y,a:0}}
 function crew(i){const c=GSVC.cur;if(!c&&i===1&&GSVC.out)return marshal();if(!c||c.phase!=='run')return null;const J=c.segs.filter(s=>s.k==='job'&&crewOf(s.j.k)===i);if(!J.length)return null;
  const A=J[0].s0,B=J[J.length-1].s1,T=c.T;if(T<A||T>B+WALK)return null;const h=crewHome(i),sp=crewSpot(c,i),P=[h,{x:sp.x,y:h.y},sp];
  if(T<A+WALK){const q=along(P,(T-A)/WALK);return{x:q.x,y:q.y,pose:'walk',a:q.a,ph:(T*1.4)%1}}
  if(T>B){const q=along(P.slice().reverse(),(T-B)/WALK);return{x:q.x,y:q.y,pose:'walk',a:q.a,ph:(T*1.4)%1}}
  const a=c.plan.at;return{x:sp.x,y:sp.y,pose:'marshal',a:Math.atan2(a.y-sp.y,a.x-sp.x),ph:(T*(i?.9:.6))%1,work:true}}
  
 function liftDraw(g,N){const c=GSVC.cur;if(!c||c.phase!=='run'||c.noLift||!c.segs.some(s=>s.k==='up'))return;const a=c.plan.at,z=c.z*K6;GSVC.n.lift++;
  const Y=N?'#8a8040':'#d9b52c',Y2=N?'#7a7238':'#f0c93a',D='#3a3f44';   
  g.save();g.translate(a.x,a.y);g.rotate(a.h);g.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,'+(.14+.14*c.z/LIFT)+')';g.fillRect(-26,-12,52,24);
  g.fillStyle=Y;g.fillRect(-22,-14,44,3);g.fillRect(-22,11,44,3);g.fillStyle=D;g.fillRect(-22,-14,2,28);g.fillRect(20,-14,2,28);g.restore();
  if(z>.3){g.save();const s=Math.sin(a.h),co=Math.cos(a.h);g.lineWidth=1.8;
   for(const side of [-12.5,12.5]){const ox=-s*side,oy=co*side;g.strokeStyle=Y;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a.x+ox-co*16,a.y+oy-s*16);g.lineTo(a.x+ox+co*16,a.y+oy+s*16-z);g.moveTo(a.x+ox+co*16,a.y+oy+s*16);g.lineTo(a.x+ox-co*16,a.y+oy-s*16-z);g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle=D;g.beginPath();g.arc(a.x+ox,a.y+oy-z/2,1.3,0,6.2832);g.fill()}
   g.translate(a.x,a.y-z);g.rotate(a.h);g.fillStyle=Y2;g.fillRect(-23,-14.5,46,3.2);g.fillRect(-23,11.3,46,3.2);g.fillStyle=D;for(const u of [-17,15])for(const v of [-13,12.7]){g.fillRect(u,v,3,1.2)}g.restore()}}
  
 function overlay(g){const c=GSVC.cur;if(!c||c.phase!=='run')return;const a=c.plan.at,z=c.z*K6,T=c.T,seg=c.seg,now=performance.now()/1000;GSVC.n.over++;
  try{if(!e3InView(a.x-60,a.y-80,120,120,30))return}catch(e){}
  g.save();
  if(seg&&seg.k==='job'){const cr=crew(crewOf(seg.j.k)),at=cr&&cr.work;
   if(at&&seg.j.k==='c'){let col='#ffffff';try{col=TurboCity.vehPal[c.neu.c][1]}catch(e){}g.fillStyle=col;
    for(let k=0;k<30;k++){const an=k*2.399+now*1.3,r=8+((k*37)%20),px=a.x+Math.cos(an)*r*.8,py=a.y-4+Math.sin(an)*r*.55;g.globalAlpha=.26+.14*Math.sin(now*3+k);g.beginPath();g.arc(px,py,2.8,0,6.2832);g.fill()}
    g.globalAlpha=.5;g.fillStyle='#ffffff';for(let k=0;k<10;k++){const an=k*2.1+now*2.2,r=4+((k*29)%14);g.beginPath();g.arc(a.x+Math.cos(an)*r*.7,a.y-6+Math.sin(an)*r*.5,1.1,0,6.2832);g.fill()}g.fillStyle=col

   else if(at){const n=seg.j.k==='r'?6:12,f={x:Math.cos(a.h),y:Math.sin(a.h)};const wx=seg.j.k==='r'?a.x-f.x*14+Math.cos(a.h+PI/2)*9:a.x,wy=seg.j.k==='r'?a.y-f.y*14+Math.sin(a.h+PI/2)*9-z:a.y-z*.45;

  const title=!seg?'':seg.k==='in'?'הרכב נכנס לתא '+c.bay:seg.k==='out'?'יוצאים מהרכב':seg.k==='up'?'המתקן מרים את הרכב':seg.k==='down'?'המתקן מוריד את הרכב':seg.j.label;
  const done=c.segs.filter(s=>s.k==='job'&&s.done).length,all=c.segs.filter(s=>s.k==='job').length,pr=Math.min(1,T/c.total),txt='תא '+c.bay+' · '+title+(seg&&seg.k==='job'?' · '+(done+1)+'/'+all:'');

  g.globalAlpha=1;g.fillStyle='#ffffff';g.font='700 4px Segoe UI,Arial';g.textAlign='center';g.textBaseline='middle';g.fillText(txt,a.x,y-.6,w-4);g.textBaseline='alphabetic';g.restore()}

 function audit(){const issues=[];try{for(const k of ['hSim','hDraw','hEnter','hLook'])if(!GSVC[k])issues.push('no-hook:'+k);const s=spot2();
   if(blocked(s.x,s.y,-PI/2))issues.push('spot-blocked');const P0={x:s.x,y:s.y+48},L=48,R={P0,P1:{x:P0.x,y:P0.y-L*.4},P2:{x:s.x,y:s.y+L*.4},P3:s};
   for(let k=1;k<16;k++){const p=bez(R,k/16);if(blocked(p.x,p.y,p.h)){issues.push('way-in-blocked');break}}
   for(const b of [2,3]){const B=bays()[b-1],cx=b===2?s.x:(B[0]+B[2])/2;if(blocked(cx,2450,-PI/2))issues.push('bay-'+b+'-blocked');
    const o={bay:b,at:{x:cx,y:2450,h:-PI/2}},X=exitPath(o);for(let k=2;k<=16;k++){const q=bez(X,k/16);if(blocked(q.x,q.y,q.h+PI)){issues.push('exit-'+b+'-blocked@'+k);break}}
    const m=mSpot(o);try{if(personBlockedAt(m.x,m.y))issues.push('marshal-'+b+'-blocked')}catch(e){}}}catch(e){issues.push('error:'+String(e&&e.message||e).slice(0,120))}
  if(GSVC.err)issues.push('err:'+GSVC.err);return{ok:!issues.length,issues,n:GSVC.n,last:GSVC.last}}

  z:+(c.z||0).toFixed(2),shown:c.shown||c.old,neu:c.neu||null,exited:!!c.exited,noLift:!!c.noLift,at:c.plan&&c.plan.at,car:{x:+PlayerCar.x.toFixed(1),y:+PlayerCar.y.toFixed(1),h:+(PlayerCar.heading||0).toFixed(2)},
  crew:[crew(0),crew(1)].map(q=>q&&{x:+q.x.toFixed(1),y:+q.y.toFixed(1),pose:q.pose,work:!!q.work}),mode:PlayerProfile.mode}}

var TCCZ={v:1,on:true,max:16,lod3Px:8.6,easeS:.35,near:320,car:false,sbcOff:true,closeRes:7.5,headK:1.36,tall:1.15,up:.3,lowK:.8,err:'',n:{face:0,looks:0,figs:0,ease:0,qaOff:0,errs:0,close:0,sbcVec:0}};
function tcczErr(e){TCCZ.n.errs++;if(!TCCZ.err)TCCZ.err=String(e&&e.stack||e).slice(0,240)}
function tcczHexRgb(h){h=String(h||'');if(/^#[0-9a-f]{3}$/i.test(h))h='#'+h[1]+h[1]+h[2]+h[2]+h[3]+h[3];if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(h))return null;const n=parseInt(h.slice(1),16);return[n>>16&255,n>>8&255,n&255]}
function tcczLight(h,k){const c=tcczHexRgb(h)||[200,200,200],f=v=>Math.round(v+(255-v)*k).toString(16).padStart(2,'0');return '#'+f(c[0])+f(c[1])+f(c[2])}    
function tcczShade(h,k){const c=tcczHexRgb(h)||[90,90,90],f=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(k>=0?v+(255-v)*k:v*(1+k))));return 'rgb('+f(c[0])+','+f(c[1])+','+f(c[2])+')'}
 
function tcczSig(L){if(L.czF)return L.czF;const h=+L.h||1.76,c=tcczHexRgb(L.hair&&L.hair.c),grey=!!c&&Math.max(...c)-Math.min(...c)<26&&Math.max(...c)>118;

function tcczFace(c,X){
 const T=c.getTransform(),pxm=Math.hypot(T.a,T.b),H=X.H,hp=H.r[2]*X.SCALE*pxm;
 if(!TCCZ.on||!(hp>=TCCZ.lod3Px))return false;
 const L=X.L,F=tcczSig(L),boy=F.a==='boy',eld=F.a==='elder',B=F.b||'none',sk=X.sk,hc=X.hairC,ux=H.r[0]/12.6,uy=H.r[2]/14.6;
 const E=Math.max(1,Math.min(1.36,1.36-(hp-9)*.012));   
 const S=(x,y)=>{const u=(x-32)/12.6*.97,v=-(y-29)/14.6*.95*(y>29&&L.czHead?TCCZ.lowK:1);    return X.at(u,Math.sqrt(Math.max(.03,1-u*u-v*v)),v)};

   try{if(State.phoneOpen)setPhoneOpen(false)}catch(e){}mount();
   if(!H.fzSet){H.fzSet=1;try{const Lk=p672(),hp=P34.heightPx(Lk,1),k=hp.px/((Lk.h||1.78)*10);H.fzK=+k.toFixed(3);if(k>.45&&k<1.1)HIN.Z.fz=k}catch(e){err('fz',e)}}
   const d=doorOut(L,b),go=()=>{H.walkIn=null;H.lv=L;H.b=b;setFloor(0,'front');H.on=true;H.n.enter++;H.el.style.display='block';H.el.__hide=false;H.panel=null;H.busy=null;H.view=null;H.fade=1;
    const n=unload();if(n)setTimeout(()=>toast('🧺 פרקת '+n+' מצרכים למזווה'),400);H.last=performance.now();cancelAnimationFrame(H.raf);H.raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop)};
   if(opt.instant)return go(),true;
   
   const P0={x:PlayerPed.x,y:PlayerPed.y},T={x:d.x,y:d.y-10},L0=Math.hypot(T.x-P0.x,T.y-P0.y);H.walkIn={t0:performance.now(),ms:Math.max(350,Math.min(1100,L0/52*1000)),P0,T,go};if(!H.raf)H.raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);return true}catch(e){err('enter',e);return false}}
 function exit(){if(!H.on)return;fadeTo(()=>{H.on=false;H.n.exit++;H.el.style.display='none';H.el.__hide=true;closePanel();H.busy=null;try{const d=doorOut(H.lv,H.b);PlayerPed.x=d.x;PlayerPed.y=d.y+8;PlayerPed.dir=4;PlayerPed.heading=Math.PI/2;PlayerPed.pose='idle';PlayerPed.moving=false;Camera.x=d.x;Camera.y=d.y}catch(e){err('exit',e)}})}
 function fadeTo(fn){const f=H.el&&H.el.querySelector('.hFade');if(!f||H.qa){fn();return}f.style.opacity='1';setTimeout(()=>{try{fn()}finally{f.style.opacity='0'}},280)}
 function setFloor(f,where){H.f=f;H.G=HIN.get(H.lv,H.b,f);const G=H.G;H.st=new Map();
  if(where==='front'&&G.front){H.x=G.doorX;H.y=G.D-.55;H.hd=-Math.PI/2}else if(G.stairs){const it=G.items.find(q=>q.k==='stairs');const a=it&&it.ap||[(G.stairs.x0+G.stairs.x1)/2,G.stairs.y1+.4];H.x=a[0];H.y=a[1];H.hd=Math.PI/2}
  else{const r=G.rooms[0];H.x=(r.x0+r.x1)/2;H.y=(r.y0+r.y1)/2}
  H.cam.x=H.x;H.cam.y=H.y;H.near=null}
 
 function openPanel(title,body){H.panel={title};const p=H.el.querySelector('.hPn');p.innerHTML=`<button class="x" data-hin="close" aria-label="סגור">✕</button><h3>${title}</h3>${body}`;p.style.display='block'}
 function closePanel(){H.panel=null;const p=H.el&&H.el.querySelector('.hPn');if(p)p.style.display='none'}
 
 function itemsOf(k){return H.G.items.filter(q=>q.k===k)}
 function stOf(it){let s=H.st.get(it);if(!s){s={};H.st.set(it,s)}return s}
 function busy(k,it,ms,o){H.busy=Object.assign({k,it,t0:performance.now(),ms},o||{});if(it)Object.assign(stOf(it),o&&o.st||{})}
 function stopBusy(){const b=H.busy;H.busy=null;if(b&&b.it){const s=stOf(b.it);s.cook=s.play=s.on=s.sleep=false;s.meal=null}if(b&&b.end)try{b.end(false)}catch(e){}}
 function standAt(p,hd){if(!p)return;H.x=p[0];H.y=p[1];if(hd!=null)H.hd=hd}
 const faceTo=it=>Math.atan2((it.y0+it.y1)/2-H.y,(it.x0+it.x1)/2-H.x);
 function act(it){const a=it.act;H.n.acts[a]=(H.n.acts[a]||0)+1;
  switch(a){
   case 'exit':return exit();
   case 'stairs':{const G=H.G;if(it.up&&it.down)return openPanel('המדרגות',`<div class="row"><button class="pr" data-hin-stairs="up">⬆ לעלות לקומה ${H.f+2}</button><button data-hin-stairs="down">⬇ לרדת לקומה ${H.f}</button></div>`);return stairs(it.up?1:-1)}
   case 'sleep':{const c=TCSLEEP.can('bed');openPanel('🛏 לישון',c.ok?TCSLEEP.card('bed','כמה זמן לישון?').replace(/<section class="pc"><h3>.*?<\/h3>/,'').replace('</section>',''):`<p>${c.why}</p>`);H.bed=it;return}
   case 'dress':{try{const s=stOf(it);s.open=true;setTimeout(()=>{s.open=false},2500);openPoiPhone('wardrobe','PLAYER-HOME')}catch(e){err('dress',e)}return}
   case 'fridge':return openPanel('🧊 המקרר והמזווה',pantryHtml());
   case 'cook':return openPanel('🍳 לבשל',cookHtml(it));
   case 'eat':return openPanel('🍽 לשבת לאכול',eatHtml(it));
   case 'sit':{const s=H.busy;if(s&&s.k==='sit'&&s.it===it)return stopBusy();sitOn(it);return}
   case 'tv':{const s=stOf(it);s.on=!s.on;if(s.on){const so=H.G.items.find(q=>(q.k==='sofa'||q.k==='sofa3')&&q.room===it.room);if(so)sitOn(so,true);openPanel('📺 החדשות של העיר',newsHtml())}else closePanel();return}
   case 'work':{const s=stOf(it);s.on=true;setTimeout(()=>{s.on=false},4000);try{setPhoneOpen(true);openPhoneApp('missions')}catch(e){err('work',e)}return}
   case 'read':return openPanel('📖 יומן המשימות',journalHtml());
   case 'trophy':return openPanel('🏆 הגביעים',trophyHtml());
   case 'shelf':return openPanel('🛍 מה שקנית בחנויות העיר',boughtHtml());
   case 'tools':return openPanel('🧰 הציוד',toolsHtml());
   case 'play':{busy('play',it,5000,{st:{play:true},end:ok=>{stOf(it).play=false;if(ok){home().hstats.played++;const me=11+((Date.now()/1000|0)%11),he=7+((Date.now()/700|0)%9);toast(it.k==='arcade'||it.k==='console'?'🎮 שיא חדש: '+(1200+me*37)+' נקודות':'🏓 ניצחת '+me+':'+Math.min(he,me-2))}}});H.hd=faceTo(it);return}
   case 'gym':{const back=[H.x,H.y];busy('gym',it,5000,{end:ok=>{H.x=back[0];H.y=back[1];if(ok)toast('💪 אימון קצר — סיימת')}});if(it.k==='tread'){H.x=(it.x0+it.x1)/2;H.y=(it.y0+it.y1)/2+.2;H.hd=-Math.PI/2}else H.hd=faceTo(it);return}
   case 'cinema':{const sc=itemsOf('screen')[0];if(sc)stOf(sc).on=true;sitOn(it,true);let ok=false;try{const k=(home().hstats.films=(home().hstats.films||0)+1);if(typeof TCSHOW!=='undefined'&&TCSHOW.open)for(const i of [k%4,0]){if(ok)break;ok=!!TCSHOW.open('film',i,{place:'הקולנוע הביתי',onEnd(){try{if(sc)stOf(sc).on=false}catch(e){}}})}}catch(e){err('film',e)}
    if(!ok)busy('film',sc||it,8000,{end:()=>{if(sc)stOf(sc).on=false;toast('🎬 ראית סרט בקולנוע הביתי')}});return}
   case 'wash':{busy('wash',it,3200,{st:{on:true},end:ok=>{stOf(it).on=false;if(ok)toast('🚿 התרעננת')}});H.hd=faceTo(it);return}
   case 'view':return viewStart(it);
   case 'house':{const s=stOf(it);s.on=true;setTimeout(()=>{s.on=false},3000);try{openPoiPhone('house','PLAYER-HOME')}catch(e){err('house',e)}return}
   case 'car':{try{if(window.__TC_GST&&__TC_GST.open)__TC_GST.open('home');else openPoiPhone('house','PLAYER-HOME')}catch(e){err('car',e)}return}
   case 'design':return openPanel('🎨 עיצוב הבית',designHtml());
  }}
 function sitOn(it,keep){const ch=it.chair||null,cx=(it.x0+it.x1)/2,cy=(it.y0+it.y1)/2;let x=cx,y=cy,hd=it.face==='N'?-Math.PI/2:it.face==='S'?Math.PI/2:it.face==='E'?0:Math.PI;
  if(it.k==='sofa'||it.k==='sofa3'){y=it.face==='N'?it.y1-.32:cy}else if(it.k==='recl'){y=it.y1-.3}else if(it.k==='fire'){const a=it.ap||[cx,cy];x=a[0];y=a[1];hd=faceTo(it)}
  H.sitFrom=[H.x,H.y];H.x=x;H.y=y;H.hd=hd;busy('sit',it,keep?1e9:1e9,{seat:1,end:()=>{if(H.sitFrom){H.x=H.sitFrom[0];H.y=H.sitFrom[1]}}})}
 function stairs(d){const nf=H.G.nf,f=H.f+d;if(f<0||f>=nf)return;closePanel();H.n.stairs++;fadeTo(()=>{setFloor(f,'stairs')})}
 function viewStart(it){closePanel();H.view={t0:performance.now(),cam:{x:Camera.x,y:Camera.y,zoom:Camera.zoom}};H.el.style.display='none';
  try{const d=doorOut(H.lv,H.b);H.view.tg={x:d.x+60,y:d.y+120,zoom:.62}}catch(e){}toast('🌇 המרפסת — Esc / F חוזרים פנימה')}
 function viewEnd(){const v=H.view;if(!v)return;H.view=null;try{Camera.x=v.cam.x;Camera.y=v.cam.y;Camera.zoom=v.cam.zoom}catch(e){}if(H.on)H.el.style.display='block'}
 function click(b){const d=b.dataset;
  if(d.hin==='close')return closePanel();if(d.hin==='exit')return exit();if(d.hin==='unload'){const n=unload();toast(n?'🧺 פרקת '+n+' מצרכים למזווה':'אין מצרכים בתיק');return openPanel('🧊 המקרר והמזווה',pantryHtml())}
  if(d.homeSleep){closePanel();const bed=H.bed;const r=TCSLEEP.go(d.homeSleep,{where:'bed',place:'בבית',after:()=>{const s=bed&&stOf(bed);if(s)s.sleep=false;if(bed&&bed.ap)standAt(bed.ap,Math.PI/2);home().hstats.slept++}});
   if(r&&r.ok&&bed){const s=stOf(bed);s.sleep=true;H.sleepBed=bed;const L=p672();s.skin=L&&L.skin||'#d9a57f';s.hair=L&&L.hair&&L.hair.c||'#2a1d14'}return}
  if(d.hinStairs)return stairs(d.hinStairs==='up'?1:-1);
  if(d.hinCook){const r=RECIPES.find(q=>q.id===d.hinCook),it=H.cookAt;if(!r||!canCook(r))return;closePanel();if(it&&it.ap)standAt(it.ap,faceTo(it));
   busy('cook',it,3600,{st:{cook:true},end:ok=>{if(it)stOf(it).cook=false;if(!ok)return;if(cookIt(r))toast('🍳 בישלת: '+r.he+' — המנה במזווה');}});return}
  if(d.hinEat){const id=d.hinEat,it=H.eatAt;if(!(pcount(id)>0))return;closePanel();const ch=it&&it.chair;if(ch){const cx=(ch.x0+ch.x1)/2,cy=(ch.y0+ch.y1)/2;H.sitFrom=[H.x,H.y];H.x=cx;H.y=cy+(ch.face==='N'?.05:ch.face==='S'?-.05:0);H.hd=faceTo(it)}
   const col=/^meal:/.test(id)?(RECIPES.find(q=>'meal:'+q.id===id)||{}).col:READY[id];const mx=ch?((ch.x0+ch.x1)/2+((it.x0+it.x1)/2))/2:(it.x0+it.x1)/2,my=ch?((ch.y0+ch.y1)/2+((it.y0+it.y1)/2))/2:(it.y0+it.y1)/2;
   busy('eat',it,4200,{seat:1,st:{meal:[mx,my],mealCol:col||'#d98a3a'},end:ok=>{if(it){stOf(it).meal=null}if(H.sitFrom){H.x=H.sitFrom[0];H.y=H.sitFrom[1]}if(!ok)return;padd(id,-1);home().hstats.meals++;H.n.eat++;toast('😋 אכלת: '+nameOf(id))}});return}
  if(d.hinDesign)return design(d.hinDesign,d.hinArg)}
 
 function pantryHtml(){const p=pantry(),ids=Object.keys(p).filter(id=>p[id]>0),bag=Object.keys(PlayerProfile.inventory||{}).filter(id=>isFood(id)&&PlayerProfile.inventory[id]>0).length;
  const li=ids.length?'<ul>'+ids.map(id=>`<li>${esc(nameOf(id))} × ${p[id]}</li>`).join('')+'</ul>':'<p>המזווה ריק. קונים מצרכים בסופר "שיבולת" (C2), בסופר השכונתי (B4), במסעדה ובבית הקפה — והם מגיעים לכאן.</p>';
  return li+`<div class="row"><button class="pr" data-hin="unload" ${bag?'':'disabled'}>🧺 פרוק קניות מהתיק${bag?' ('+bag+')':''}</button></div><small>מכאן מבשלים במטבח ואוכלים בפינת האוכל.</small>`}
 function cookHtml(it){H.cookAt=it;const rows=RECIPES.map(r=>{const ok=canCook(r),need=r.any?'ארוחה מוכנה מהמסעדה / מהקפה':Object.entries(r.need).map(([id,k])=>nameOf(id)+(k>1?' × '+k:'')+(pcount(id)>=k?' ✓':'')).join(' + ');

 function eatHtml(it){H.eatAt=it;const ids=edible();if(!ids.length)return'<p>אין מה לאכול במזווה. מבשלים במטבח, או קונים ארוחה מוכנה במסעדה / בקפה.</p>';

 function cups(){let n=0;try{n+=(PlayerProfile.progression.completedMissions||[]).length}catch(e){}try{const A=PlayerProfile.achievements||PlayerProfile.progression.achievements;if(Array.isArray(A))n+=A.length;else if(A&&typeof A==='object')n+=Object.keys(A).length}catch(e){}return n}

 function design(k,v){const h=home();if(k==='car'){closePanel();try{__TC_GST.open('home')}catch(e){err('gst',e)}return}if(k==='reset'){delete h.design}else{h.design=Object.assign({},h.design||{},{[k]:v})}applyDesign();openPanel('🎨 עיצוב הבית',designHtml())}
 function applyDesign(){const d=home().design||null;for(const G of [H.G]){if(!G)continue;if(!G.P0)G.P0=Object.assign({},G.P);Object.assign(G.P,G.P0);if(d&&G.site.b!=='S'){if(d.wall)G.P.wall=d.wall;if(d.floor){G.P.floor=d.floor}}}}
 
 function p672(){try{return p672Pl()}catch(e){return null}}
 function F(){if(!H.on)return false;if(H.view){viewEnd();return true}if(H.panel){closePanel();return true}if(H.busy){if(H.busy.k==='sleep')return true;stopBusy();return true}if(H.near)act(H.near);return true}
 function step(dt,now){const G=H.G;if(!G)return;
  
  if(H.busy&&H.busy.ms<1e8&&now-H.busy.t0>H.busy.ms){const b=H.busy;H.busy=null;try{b.end&&b.end(true)}catch(e){err('end',e)}}
  if(TCSLEEP.S.busy||H.panel||State.phoneOpen||State.mapOpen){H.moving=false;return}
  const dx=(Input.down('KeyD','ArrowRight')?1:0)-(Input.down('KeyA','ArrowLeft')?1:0),dy=(Input.down('KeyS','ArrowDown')?1:0)-(Input.down('KeyW','ArrowUp')?1:0),m=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
  if(m>.01&&H.busy&&H.busy.k!=='sleep')stopBusy();
  if(m>.01&&!H.busy){const run=Input.down('ShiftLeft','ShiftRight'),sp=(run?3.1:1.65)*dt,ux=dx/m,uy=dy/m;H.hd=Math.atan2(uy,ux);H.run=run;let mv=0;
   const nx=H.x+ux*sp,ny=H.y+uy*sp;if(!HIN.blocked(G,nx,ny)){H.x=nx;H.y=ny;mv=sp}else{if(!HIN.blocked(G,nx,H.y)){H.x=nx;mv=Math.abs(ux*sp)}else if(!HIN.blocked(G,H.x,ny)){H.y=ny;mv=Math.abs(uy*sp)}}
   H.dist+=mv;H.moving=mv>0}else H.moving=false;
  
  let best=null,bd=.75;if(!H.busy)for(const it of G.items){if(!it.act||!it.ap)continue;const d=Math.hypot(H.x-it.ap[0],H.y-it.ap[1]);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=it}}H.near=best}
 function prompt(){const el=H.el.querySelector('.hPr');let t='';if(H.busy&&H.busy.k!=='sleep')t='<kbd>F</kbd>'+(H.busy.k==='sit'?'לקום':'להפסיק');else if(H.near&&!H.panel){const it=H.near;t='<kbd>F</kbd>'+HIN.label(it,H.f)}
  if(el.__t!==t){el.__t=t;el.innerHTML=t;el.style.display=t?'block':'none'}}
 function top(){const G=H.G,r=G.rooms.find(q=>H.x>=q.x0&&H.x<=q.x1&&H.y>=q.y0&&H.y<=q.y1);let c='';try{const k=gameClock();c='יום '+k.day+' · '+k.hhmm}catch(e){}

  return L; }
 function post(L, h, b) { L.news.unshift({ k: 'sport', h, b, g: gmin() }); if (L.news.length > 40) L.news.length = 40; }

 function kitPair(h, a) { const H = Object.assign({}, T[h]), A = Object.assign({}, T[a]); if (cd(H.shirt, A.shirt) < 120) { A.shirt = A.alt[0]; A.shorts = A.alt[1]; A.num = A.alt[2]; } return [H, A]; }
 function table(L, RES) { const rows = T.map((x, t) => ({ t, p: 0, w: 0, l: 0, pf: 0, pa: 0, pts: 0 }));
  for (const R of (RES || L.res)) if (R) for (const m of R.m) { const A = rows[m.h], B = rows[m.a], x = m.g[0], y = m.g[1], hw = x > y || (x === y && m.ft && m.ft[0] > m.ft[1]); A.p++; B.p++; A.pf += x; A.pa += y; B.pf += y; B.pa += x; if (hw) { A.w++; B.l++; A.pts += 2; B.pts += 1; } else { B.w++; A.l++; B.pts += 2; A.pts += 1; } }
  return rows.sort((a, b) => b.pts - a.pts || (b.pf - b.pa) - (a.pf - a.pa) || b.pf - a.pf || a.t - b.t); }
 function fx(L) { if (L.round < 5) { const m = R5[L.round][SLOT(L.round)]; return { kind: 'r', r: L.round, h: m[0], a: m[1] }; } const t = table(L); return { kind: 'f', r: 5, h: t[0].t, a: t[1].t }; }

 function playRound(L, g, ft, day) { const r = L.round, s = L.season, rs = R5[r].map((m, i) => { if (i === SLOT(r)) return { h: m[0], a: m[1], g: g.slice(), ft: ft || null, st: 1 }; const R = rngL(s * 997 + r * 31 + i * 7 + 5); let x = pts(R, 10), y = pts(R, 9); if (x === y) x++; return { h: m[0], a: m[1], g: [x, y], st: 0 }; });

 function crown(L, h, a, g, ft, day) { const w = g[0] !== g[1] ? (g[0] > g[1] ? h : a) : (ft && ft[0] > ft[1] ? h : a); L.fin = { s: L.season, h, a, g, ft, w, day: day || null, g0: gmin() };

 function finish(idx, g, ft) { const L = st(), B = (L.bind && L.bind.idx === idx && L.bind) || MEMO.get(idx) || FORCE.get(idx); if (!B || B.done) return false; B.done = true;

  if (idx < 8000) L.lastDay = Math.max(L.lastDay, idx); return true; }
 function catchUp(L, g) { const day = dayOf(g); let n = 0;

   const live = L.bind && L.bind.idx === d; if (live && L.bind.done) { L.lastDay = d; continue; }

 function audit() { const out = { ok: true, why: [] }, bad = w => { out.ok = false; out.why.push(w); };
